.9020						escape_tr_table:
>9020		07				    .byte   7               ; a -> BEL (ASCII value 7)
>9021		08				    .byte   8               ; b -> Backspace (ASCII value 8)
>9022		00 00				    .byte   0,0             ; c, d no escape
>9024		1b				    .byte   27              ; e -> ESC (ASCII value 27)
>9025		0c				    .byte   12              ; f -> FF (ASCII value 12)
>9026		00 00 00 00 00			    .byte   0,0,0,0,0       ; g,h,i,j,k
>902b		0a				    .byte   10              ; l -> LF (ASCII value 10)
>902c		8d				    .byte   13+128          ; m -> CR/LF pair (ASCII values 13, 10)
>902d		0a				    .byte   10              ; n behaves like l --> lf
>902e		00 00				    .byte   0,0             ; o,p
>9030		22				    .byte   34              ; q -> Double quote (ASCII value 34)
>9031		0d				    .byte   13              ; r ->  CR (ASCII value 13)
>9032		00				    .byte   0               ; s
>9033		09				    .byte   9               ; t -> Horizontal TAB (ASCII value 9)
>9034		00				    .byte   0               ; u
>9035		0b				    .byte   11              ; v -> Vertical TAB (ASCII value 11)
>9036		00 00 00			    .byte   0,0,0           ; w,x,y   (x is a special case later)
>9039		80				    .byte   0+128           ; z -> NULL (ASCII value 0)

;******  Processing file: platform/../definitions.asm

=$0000						ram_start = $0000           ; start of installed RAM, must include zpage
=$0000						zpage     = ram_start       ; begin of Zero Page usage ($0000-$00ff)
=$0100						stack0    = $0100           ; begin of Return Stack ($0100-$01ff)
=$7f						zpage_end = $7F             ; last byte (inclusive) of Zero Page reserved for Tali ($0000-$007f)
=32767						ram_end   = $8000-1         ; end of installed RAM
=31744						hist_buff = ram_end-$03ff   ; begin of history buffers
=$ff						rsp0      = $ff             ; initial Return Stack Pointer (65c02 stack)
=$ff						bsize     = $ff             ; size of input/output buffers
=$0000						user0     = zpage           ; start of TaliForth2 system variables
=512						buffer0   = stack0+$100     ; input buffer ($0200-$02ff)
=768						cp0       = buffer0+bsize+1 ; Dictionary starts after last buffer
=31744						cp_end    = hist_buff       ; Last RAM byte available for code
=$ff						padoffset = $ff             ; offset from CP to PAD (holds number strings)
=120						dsp0      = zpage_end-7     ; initial Data Stack Pointer, used to initialize the X register.
.b333						cold_zp_table:
>b333	0000	00 08				cp:         .word cp0+256+1024      ; Compiler Pointer
>b335	0002	67 c0				dp:         .word dictionary_start  ; Dictionary Pointer
>b337	0004	00 00				ip:         .word 0                 ; Instruction Pointer (current xt)
>b339	0006	00 00				workword:   .word 0                 ; nt (not xt!) of word being compiled, except in
>b33b	0008	00 03				up:         .word cp0               ; Forth user vars at start of available RAM
>b33d	000a	00 00				insrc:      .word 0                 ; input source for SOURCE-ID (0 for keyboard)
>b33f	000c	00 02				cib:        .word buffer0           ; address of current input buffer
